## vin02m1.mul.txt | Piṇḍo gaṇaṃ paraṃ pūvaṃ, dve ca vuttā pavāraṇā;
old: ['piṇḍo gaṇaṃ paraṃ pūvaṃ,\ndve ca vuttā pavāraṇā,\nmahānāmo pasenadi,\nsenāviddho ime dasāti [acelakaṃ uyyojañca,\nsabhojanaṃ duve raho,\nsabhattakañca bhesajjaṃ,\nuyyuttaṃ senuyyodhikaṃ].']
new: ['piṇḍo gaṇaṃ paraṃ pūvaṃ,\ndve ca vuttā pavāraṇā,\nvikāle sannidhī khīraṃ,\ndantaponena te dasāti.']

new: ['vikāle sannidhī khīraṃ,\ndantaponena te dasāti.']
